============================================================
FILE: mob/app/_layout.js
============================================================

     1	import { Stack, usePathname } from "expo-router";
     2	import { useFonts } from "expo-font";
     3	import { Animated, View } from "react-native";
     4	import { useRef } from "react";
     5	
     6	import AppHeader from "../components/AppHeader";
     7	import ScreenFade from "../components/ScreenFade";
     8	import { HeaderScrollProvider } from "../utils/headerScrollContext";
     9	
    10	export default function RootLayout() {
    11	  const pathname = usePathname();
    12	  const headerScrollY = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
    13	
    14	  const [fontsLoaded] = useFonts({
    15	    "Dream Avenue": require("../assets/fonts/dream_avenue/dream_avenue.ttf"),
    16	    "TT Fors": require("../assets/fonts/tt_fors/tt_fors_trial_regular.ttf"),
    17	  });
    18	
    19	  if (!fontsLoaded) {
    20	    return null;
    21	  }
    22	
    23	  const showPersistentHeader = pathname !== "/shop";
    24	
    25	  const useOverlayHeader =
    26	    pathname === "/" ||
    27	    pathname === "/products" ||
    28	    pathname === "/aboutus" ||
    29	    pathname === "/contact";
    30	
    31	  return (
    32	    <HeaderScrollProvider scrollY={headerScrollY}>
    33	      <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: "#FFFCF2" }}>
    34	        <Stack
    35	          screenOptions={{
    36	            headerShown: false,
    37	            contentStyle: {
    38	              backgroundColor: "#FFFCF2",
    39	            },
    40	          }}
    41	        />
    42	
    43	        <ScreenFade />
    44	
    45	        {showPersistentHeader && useOverlayHeader ? (
    46	          <View
    47	            pointerEvents="box-none"
    48	            style={{
    49	              position: "absolute",
    50	              top: 0,
    51	              left: 0,
    52	              right: 0,
    53	              zIndex: 1000000,
    54	              elevation: 1000000,
    55	            }}
    56	          >
    57	            <AppHeader scrollY={headerScrollY} />
    58	          </View>
    59	        ) : null}
    60	
    61	        {showPersistentHeader && !useOverlayHeader ? (
    62	          <AppHeader scrollY={headerScrollY} />
    63	        ) : null}
    64	      </View>
    65	    </HeaderScrollProvider>
    66	  );
    67	}

     1	import { Animated, Image, Text, View } from "react-native";

    89	function ProductSection({ product }) {
    90	  return (
    91	    <View style={productsStyles.productCard}>
    92	      <View style={productsStyles.productImageWrap}>
    93	        <Image
    94	          source={product.image}
    95	          style={productsStyles.productImage}
    96	          resizeMode="contain"
    97	          accessibilityLabel={product.alt}
    98	        />
    99	      </View>
   100	
   101	      <Text style={productsStyles.productTitle}>{product.title}</Text>
   102	      <Text style={productsStyles.productDescription}>{product.serving}</Text>
   103	
   104	      <View style={productsStyles.productDetails}>
   105	        {product.includes.map((section) => (
   106	          <View key={section.title} style={productsStyles.includeSection}>
   107	            <View style={productsStyles.includeHeaderRow}>
   108	              <Text style={productsStyles.includeTitle}>{section.title}</Text>
   109	              <Text style={productsStyles.includeAmount}>{section.amount}</Text>
   110	            </View>
   111	
   112	            {section.items.map((item) => (
   113	              <Text key={item} style={productsStyles.includeItem}>
   114	                → {item}
   115	              </Text>
   116	            ))}
   117	
   118	            {section.note ? (
   119	              <Text style={productsStyles.includeNote}>{section.note}</Text>
   120	            ) : null}
   121	          </View>
   122	        ))}
   123	      </View>
   124	    </View>
   125	  );
   126	}
   127	
   128	export default function ProductsScreen() {
   129	  const scrollY = useHeaderScrollY();
   130	
   131	  useEffect(() => {
   132	    scrollY?.setValue(0);
   133	  }, [scrollY]);
   134	
   135	  return (
   136	    <View style={productsStyles.screen}>
   137	      <Animated.ScrollView
   138	        style={productsStyles.scroll}
   139	        contentContainerStyle={productsStyles.scrollContent}
   140	        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
   141	        scrollEventThrottle={16}
   142	        onScroll={Animated.event(
   143	          [{ nativeEvent: { contentOffset: { y: scrollY } } }],
   144	          { useNativeDriver: false }
   145	        )}
   146	      >
   147	        <View style={productsStyles.main}>
   148	          <Text style={productsStyles.pageTitle}> </Text>
   149	
   150	          <PageDivider />
   151	
   152	          {products.map((product, index) => (
   153	            <View key={product.title}>
   154	              <ProductSection product={product} />
   155	              {index < products.length - 1 ? <PageDivider /> : null}
   156	            </View>
   157	          ))}
   158	        </View>
   159	      </Animated.ScrollView>
   160	    </View>
   161	  );
   162	}

============================================================
FILE: mob/components/AppHeader.js
============================================================

     1	import {
     2	  Animated,
     3	  PanResponder,
     4	  Platform,
     5	  Pressable,
     6	  Text,
     7	  View,
     8	} from "react-native";
     9	import { router, usePathname } from "expo-router";
    10	import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
    11	
    12	import styles from "../styles/headerStyles";
    13	
    14	const navPages = ["home", "products", "aboutus", "contact"];
    15	
    16	const pageLabels = {
    17	  home: "Home",
    18	  products: "Products",
    19	  aboutus: "About Us",
    20	  contact: "Contact",
    21	  shop: "Shop",
    22	};
    23	
    24	const pageRoutes = {
    25	  home: "/",
    26	  products: "/products",
    27	  aboutus: "/aboutus",
    28	  contact: "/contact",
    29	  shop: "/shop",
    30	};
    31	
    32	function getActivePageFromPath(pathname) {
    33	  if (pathname === "/products") return "products";
    34	  if (pathname === "/aboutus") return "aboutus";
    35	  if (pathname === "/contact") return "contact";
    36	  if (pathname === "/shop") return "shop";
    37	  return "home";
    38	}
    39	
    40	export default function AppHeader({
    41	  activePage,
    42	  scrollY,
    43	  showCarousel = true,
    44	  showHero = true,
    45	  showOnlyCarousel = false,
    46	  showOnlyHero = false,
    47	}) {
    48	  const pathname = usePathname();
    49	  const resolvedActivePage = activePage || getActivePageFromPath(pathname);
    50	
    51	  const fallbackScrollY = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
    52	  const safeScrollY = scrollY || fallbackScrollY;
    53	
    54	  const leftArrowX = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
    55	  const rightArrowX = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
    56	  const arrowOpacity = useRef(new Animated.Value(0.15)).current;
    57	  const stickyPadding = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
    58	  const swipeTextTranslateX = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
    59	
    60	  const arrowLoopRef = useRef(null);
    61	  const wasAtTopRef = useRef(true);
    62	  const isStickyRef = useRef(false);
    63	
    64	  const activePageRef = useRef(resolvedActivePage);
    65	  const activeIndexRef = useRef(Math.max(navPages.indexOf(resolvedActivePage), 0));
    66	
    67	  activePageRef.current = resolvedActivePage;
    68	  activeIndexRef.current = Math.max(navPages.indexOf(resolvedActivePage), 0);
    69	
    70	  useEffect(() => {
    71	    swipeTextTranslateX.setValue(0);
    72	  }, [resolvedActivePage, swipeTextTranslateX]);
    73	
    74	  const goToPage = (pageName) => {
    75	    if (pageName === activePageRef.current) return;
    76	
    77	    const route = pageRoutes[pageName];
    78	    if (route) router.push(route);
    79	  };
    80	
    81	  const goToPreviousPage = () => {
    82	    const previousIndex =
    83	      (activeIndexRef.current + navPages.length - 1) % navPages.length;
    84	
    85	    goToPage(navPages[previousIndex]);
    86	  };
    87	
    88	  const goToNextPage = () => {
    89	    const nextIndex = (activeIndexRef.current + 1) % navPages.length;
    90	
    91	    goToPage(navPages[nextIndex]);
    92	  };
    93	
    94	  const carouselPanResponder = useRef(
    95	    PanResponder.create({
    96	      onMoveShouldSetPanResponder: (_, gestureState) =>
    97	        Math.abs(gestureState.dx) > 22 &&
    98	        Math.abs(gestureState.dx) > Math.abs(gestureState.dy),
    99	
   100	      onPanResponderMove: (_, gestureState) => {
   101	        swipeTextTranslateX.setValue(gestureState.dx);
   102	      },
   103	
   104	      onPanResponderRelease: (_, gestureState) => {
   105	        if (gestureState.dx <= -45) {
   106	          Animated.timing(swipeTextTranslateX, {
   107	            toValue: -260,
   108	            duration: 210,
   109	            useNativeDriver: true,
   110	          }).start(() => {
   111	            goToNextPage();
   112	          });
   113	
   114	          return;
   115	        }
   116	
   117	        if (gestureState.dx >= 45) {
   118	          Animated.timing(swipeTextTranslateX, {
   119	            toValue: 260,
   120	            duration: 210,
   121	            useNativeDriver: true,
   122	          }).start(() => {
   123	            goToPreviousPage();
   124	          });
   125	
   126	          return;
   127	        }
   128	
   129	        Animated.spring(swipeTextTranslateX, {
   130	          toValue: 0,
   131	          useNativeDriver: true,
   132	          tension: 52,
   133	          friction: 7,
   134	        }).start();
   135	      },
   136	
   137	      onPanResponderTerminate: () => {
   138	        Animated.spring(swipeTextTranslateX, {
   139	          toValue: 0,
   140	          useNativeDriver: true,
   141	          tension: 52,
   142	          friction: 7,
   143	        }).start();
   144	      },
   145	    })
   146	  ).current;
   147	
   148	  useEffect(() => {
   149	    const startArrowLoop = () => {
   150	      if (arrowLoopRef.current) arrowLoopRef.current.stop();
   151	
   152	      leftArrowX.setValue(0);
   153	      rightArrowX.setValue(0);
   154	      arrowOpacity.setValue(0.15);
   155	
   156	      arrowLoopRef.current = Animated.loop(
   157	        Animated.sequence([
   158	          Animated.parallel([
   159	            Animated.timing(leftArrowX, {
   160	              toValue: 0,
   161	              duration: 0,
   162	              useNativeDriver: true,
   163	            }),
   164	            Animated.timing(rightArrowX, {
   165	              toValue: 0,
   166	              duration: 0,
   167	              useNativeDriver: true,
   168	            }),
   169	            Animated.timing(arrowOpacity, {
   170	              toValue: 0.15,
   171	              duration: 0,
   172	              useNativeDriver: true,
   173	            }),
   174	          ]),
   175	
   176	          Animated.timing(arrowOpacity, {
   177	            toValue: 0.45,
   178	            duration: 490,
   179	            useNativeDriver: true,
   180	          }),
   181	
   182	          Animated.timing(arrowOpacity, {
   183	            toValue: 0.15,
   184	            duration: 490,
   185	            useNativeDriver: true,
   186	          }),
   187	
   188	          Animated.timing(arrowOpacity, {
   189	            toValue: 0.45,
   190	            duration: 490,
   191	            useNativeDriver: true,
   192	          }),
   193	
   194	          Animated.timing(arrowOpacity, {
   195	            toValue: 0.15,
   196	            duration: 490,
   197	            useNativeDriver: true,
   198	          }),
   199	
   200	          Animated.timing(arrowOpacity, {
   201	            toValue: 0.45,
   202	            duration: 490,
   203	            useNativeDriver: true,
   204	          }),
   205	
   206	          Animated.parallel([
   207	            Animated.timing(arrowOpacity, {
   208	              toValue: 0,
   209	              duration: 665,
   210	              useNativeDriver: true,
   211	            }),
   212	            Animated.timing(leftArrowX, {
   213	              toValue: -120,
   214	              duration: 665,
   215	              useNativeDriver: true,
   216	            }),
   217	            Animated.timing(rightArrowX, {
   218	              toValue: 120,
   219	              duration: 665,
   220	              useNativeDriver: true,
   221	            }),
   222	          ]),
   223	
   224	          Animated.delay(500),
   225	        ])
   226	      );
   227	
   228	      arrowLoopRef.current.start();
   229	    };
   230	
   231	    const stopArrowLoop = () => {
   232	      if (arrowLoopRef.current) arrowLoopRef.current.stop();
   233	
   234	      leftArrowX.setValue(0);
   235	      rightArrowX.setValue(0);
   236	      arrowOpacity.setValue(0);
   237	    };
   238	
   239	    const animateStickyPadding = (toValue) => {
   240	      Animated.timing(stickyPadding, {
   241	        toValue,
   242	        duration: 90,
   243	        useNativeDriver: false,
   244	      }).start();
   245	    };
   246	
   247	    startArrowLoop();
   248	
   249	    const listenerId = safeScrollY.addListener(({ value }) => {
   250	      const isAtTop = value <= 1;
   251	      const shouldBeSticky = value >= 120 && Platform.OS !== "web";
   252	
   253	      if (shouldBeSticky !== isStickyRef.current) {
   254	        isStickyRef.current = shouldBeSticky;
   255	        animateStickyPadding(shouldBeSticky ? 20 : 0);
   256	      }
   257	
   258	      if (!isAtTop && wasAtTopRef.current) {
   259	        wasAtTopRef.current = false;
   260	        stopArrowLoop();
   261	      }
   262	
   263	      if (isAtTop && !wasAtTopRef.current) {
   264	        wasAtTopRef.current = true;
   265	        startArrowLoop();
   266	      }
   267	    });
   268	
   269	    return () => {
   270	      stopArrowLoop();
   271	      safeScrollY.removeListener(listenerId);
   272	    };
   273	  }, [arrowOpacity, leftArrowX, rightArrowX, safeScrollY, stickyPadding]);
   274	
   275	  const activeLink = pageLabels[resolvedActivePage] || "Home";
   276	
   277	  const arrowPlacementOpacity = safeScrollY.interpolate({
   278	    inputRange: [0, 1],
   279	    outputRange: [1, 0],
   280	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   281	  });
   282	
   283	  const visibleArrowOpacity = Animated.multiply(
   284	    arrowOpacity,
   285	    arrowPlacementOpacity
   286	  );
   287	
   288	  const stickyHeight =
   289	    Platform.OS === "web" ? 84 : Animated.add(84, stickyPadding);
   290	
   291	  const centerShadowOpacity =
   292	    Platform.OS === "web"
   293	      ? 1
   294	      : stickyPadding.interpolate({
   295	          inputRange: [0, 20],
   296	          outputRange: [0, 1],
   297	          extrapolate: "clamp",
   298	        });
   299	
   300	  const heroScale = safeScrollY.interpolate({
   301	    inputRange: [0, 500],
   302	    outputRange: [1.5, 3.05],
   303	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   304	  });
   305	
   306	  const heroTranslateY = safeScrollY.interpolate({
   307	    inputRange: [0, 500],
   308	    outputRange: [30, -56],
   309	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   310	  });
   311	
   312	  const headerTranslateY = safeScrollY.interpolate({
   313	    inputRange: [0, 120],
   314	    outputRange: [0, -120],
   315	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   316	  });
   317	
   318	  const carousel = (
   319	    <View style={styles.carouselShell} {...carouselPanResponder.panHandlers}>
   320	      <Animated.View
   321	        style={[
   322	          styles.carouselNavBar,
   323	          Platform.OS !== "web"
   324	            ? {
   325	                height: stickyHeight,
   326	                paddingTop: stickyPadding,
   327	              }
   328	            : null,
   329	        ]}
   330	      >
   331	        <View style={styles.carouselInner}>
   332	          <Pressable onPress={goToPreviousPage}>
   333	            <Animated.View
   334	              style={[
   335	                styles.arrowBox,
   336	                {
   337	                  opacity: visibleArrowOpacity,
   338	                  transform: [{ translateX: leftArrowX }],
   339	                },
   340	              ]}
   341	            >
   342	              <View style={[styles.arrowChevron, styles.arrowChevronLeft]} />
   343	            </Animated.View>
   344	          </Pressable>
   345	
   346	          <Pressable onPress={goToNextPage} style={styles.carouselActiveWrap}>
   347	            <Animated.Text
   348	              style={[
   349	                styles.carouselActiveText,
   350	                {
   351	                  transform: [{ translateX: swipeTextTranslateX }],
   352	                },
   353	              ]}
   354	            >
   355	              {activeLink}
   356	            </Animated.Text>
   357	
   358	            <View pointerEvents="none" style={styles.carouselIndicatorRow}>
   359	              {navPages.map((pageName) => (
   360	                <View
   361	                  key={`carousel-indicator-${pageName}`}
   362	                  style={[
   363	                    styles.carouselIndicatorDot,
   364	                    pageName === resolvedActivePage &&
   365	                      styles.carouselIndicatorDotActive,
   366	                  ]}
   367	                />
   368	              ))}
   369	            </View>
   370	          </Pressable>
   371	
   372	          <Pressable onPress={goToNextPage}>
   373	            <Animated.View
   374	              style={[
   375	                styles.arrowBox,
   376	                {
   377	                  opacity: visibleArrowOpacity,
   378	                  transform: [{ translateX: rightArrowX }],
   379	                },
   380	              ]}
   381	            >
   382	              <View style={[styles.arrowChevron, styles.arrowChevronRight]} />
   383	            </Animated.View>
   384	          </Pressable>
   385	        </View>
   386	      </Animated.View>
   387	
   388	      <Animated.View
   389	        pointerEvents="none"
   390	        style={[
   391	          styles.carouselCenterShadow,
   392	          {
   393	            opacity: centerShadowOpacity,
   394	          },
   395	        ]}
   396	      >
   397	        {Platform.OS !== "web" && (
   398	          <>
   399	            {Array.from({ length: 36 }, (_, index) => (
   400	              <View
   401	                key={`center-shadow-layer-${index + 1}`}
   402	                style={styles[`centerShadowLayer${index + 1}`]}
   403	              />
   404	            ))}
   405	          </>
   406	        )}
   407	      </Animated.View>
   408	    </View>
   409	  );
   410	
   411	  const hero = (
   412	    <View style={styles.hero} {...carouselPanResponder.panHandlers}>
   413	      <Animated.Image
   414	        source={require("../background3.png")}
   415	        style={[
   416	          styles.heroImage,
   417	          {
   418	            transform: [{ scale: heroScale }, { translateY: heroTranslateY }],
   419	          },
   420	        ]}
   421	        resizeMode="cover"
   422	      />
   423	    </View>
   424	  );
   425	
   426	  if (showOnlyCarousel) return carousel;
   427	  if (showOnlyHero) return hero;
   428	
   429	  return (
   430	    <Animated.View
   431	      style={[
   432	        styles.header,
   433	        {
   434	          transform: [{ translateY: headerTranslateY }],
   435	        },
   436	      ]}
   437	    >
   438	      <View style={styles.orangeBar}>
   439	        <Pressable style={styles.logoPressable} onPress={() => goToPage("home")}>
   440	          <Text style={styles.logoText}>Alla Vostra</Text>
   441	        </Pressable>
   442	
   443	        <Pressable style={styles.shopButtonWrap} onPress={() => goToPage("shop")}>
   444	          <View style={styles.shopButton}>
   445	            <Text style={styles.shopButtonText}>SHOP</Text>
   446	          </View>
   447	        </Pressable>
   448	      </View>
   449	
   450	      {showCarousel && carousel}
   451	      {showHero && hero}
   452	    </Animated.View>
   453	  );
   454	}

FILE: mob/index-old.html
============================================================

     1	<script>
     2	
     3	const parallax =
     4	  document.querySelector(".parallax");
     5	
     6	const navbar =
     7	  document.getElementById("indexTable");
     8	
     9	let frozen = false;
    10	
    11	function updateFreeze() {
    12	
    13	  if (!parallax || !navbar) return;
    14	
    15	  const navbarRect =
    16	    navbar.getBoundingClientRect();
    17	
    18	  const navbarBottom =
    19	    navbarRect.bottom;
    20	
    21	  /*
    22	    freeze exactly when navbar
    23	    leaves viewport
    24	  */
    25	
    26	  if (navbarBottom <= 0 && !frozen) {
    27	
    28	    frozen = true;
    29	
    30	    /*
    31	      capture current visible state
    32	    */
    33	
    34	    const rect =
    35	      parallax.getBoundingClientRect();
    36	
    37	    /*
    38	      permanently pin hero/banner
    39	    */
    40	
    41	    parallax.style.position = "fixed";
    42	
    43	    parallax.style.top =
    44	      rect.top + "px";
    45	
    46	    parallax.style.left =
    47	      rect.left + "px";
    48	
    49	    parallax.style.width =
    50	      rect.width + "px";
    51	
    52	    parallax.style.height =
    53	      rect.height + "px";
    54	
    55	    parallax.style.margin = "0";
    56	
    57	    parallax.style.zIndex = "1";
    58	
    59	    parallax.style.pointerEvents =
    60	      "none";
    61	
    62	    /*
    63	      freeze all transforms
    64	    */
    65	
    66	    parallax
    67	      .querySelectorAll("*")
    68	      .forEach(el => {
    69	
    70	        const computed =
    71	          getComputedStyle(el).transform;
    72	
    73	        if (
    74	          computed &&
    75	          computed !== "none"
    76	        ) {
    77	          el.style.transform =
    78	            computed;
    79	        }
    80	
    81	      });
    82	
    83	    parallax.classList.add("frozen");
    84	
    85	  }
    86	
    87	}
    88	
    89	/*
    90	  disable ALL further animations/transitions
    91	*/
    92	
    93	const style =
    94	  document.createElement("style");
    95	
    96	style.innerHTML = `
    97	
    98	.parallax.frozen *,
    99	.parallax.frozen svg,
   100	.parallax.frozen div {
   101	
   102	  animation: none !important;
   103	
   104	  transition: none !important;
   105	
   106	  will-change: auto !important;
   107	
   108	}
   109	
   110	`;
   111	
   112	document.head.appendChild(style);
   113	
   114	/*
   115	  monitor scrolling
   116	*/
   117	
   118	window.addEventListener(
   119	  "scroll",
   120	  updateFreeze,
   121	  { passive: true }
   122	);
   123	
   124	/*
   125	  original autoscroll behavior
   126	*/
   127	
   128	window.addEventListener("click", () => {
   129	
   130	  window.scrollTo({
   131	    top: 450,
   132	    behavior: "smooth",
   133	  });
   134	
   135	});
   136	
   137	</script>

FILE: mob/script.js
============================================================

     1	"use strict";
     2	//  passive events 
     3	let passiveSupported = false;
     4	try {
     5	    window.addEventListener("test", null, Object.defineProperty({}, "passive", { get: function () { passiveSupported = true; } }));
     6	}
     7	catch (err) { }
     8	//  scroll listener
     9	addEventListener('scroll', e => {
    10	    const height = document.querySelector('.parallax').getBoundingClientRect().height;
    11	    const percentage = Math.min(Math.max(pageYOffset / height, 0), 1);
    12	    document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--pct', percentage);
    13	}, passiveSupported ? { passive: true } : false);
    14	//  link to open without iframe 
    15	const inIframe = () => { try {
    16	    return window.self !== window.top;
    17	}
    18	catch (e) {
    19	    return true;
    20	} };
    21	if (inIframe())
    22	    document.querySelector('.no-iframe').style.display = 'block';
    23	
    24	
    25	
    26	
    27	// contact form
    28	
    29	const form = document.getElementById("form");
    30	const result = document.getElementById("result");
    31	
    32	form.addEventListener("submit", function (e) {
    33	  const formData = new FormData(form);
    34	  e.preventDefault();
    35	  var object = {};
    36	  formData.forEach((value, key) => {
    37	    object[key] = value;
    38	  });
    39	  var json = JSON.stringify(object);
    40	  result.innerHTML = "Please wait...";
    41	
    42	  fetch("https://api.web3forms.com/submit", {
    43	    method: "POST",
    44	    headers: {
    45	      "Content-Type": "application/json",
    46	      Accept: "application/json"
    47	    },
    48	    body: json
    49	  })
    50	    .then(async (response) => {
    51	      let json = await response.json();
    52	      if (response.status == 200) {
    53	        result.innerHTML = json.message;
    54	        result.classList.remove("text-gray-500");
    55	        result.classList.add("text-green-500");
    56	      } else {
    57	        console.log(response);
    58	        result.innerHTML = json.message;
    59	        result.classList.remove("text-gray-500");
    60	        result.classList.add("text-red-500");
    61	      }
    62	    })
    63	    .catch((error) => {
    64	      console.log(error);
    65	      result.innerHTML = "Something went wrong!";
    66	    })
    67	    .then(function () {
    68	      form.reset();
    69	      setTimeout(() => {
    70	        result.style.display = "none";
    71	      }, 5000);
    72	    });
    73	});
    74	
    75	// end of contact form
    76

FILE: mob/shop.js
============================================================

     1	if (document.readyState == 'loading') {
     2	    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', ready)
     3	} else {
     4	    ready()
     5	}
     6	
     7	function ready() {
     8	    var removeCartItemButtons = document.getElementsByClassName('btn-danger')
     9	    for (var i = 0; i < removeCartItemButtons.length; i++) {
    10	        var button = removeCartItemButtons[i]
    11	        button.addEventListener('click', removeCartItem)
    12	    }
    13	
    14	    var quantityInputs = document.getElementsByClassName('cart-quantity-input')
    15	    for (var i = 0; i < quantityInputs.length; i++) {
    16	        var input = quantityInputs[i]
    17	        input.addEventListener('change', quantityChanged)
    18	    }
    19	
    20	    var addToCartButtons = document.getElementsByClassName('shop-item-button')
    21	    for (var i = 0; i < addToCartButtons.length; i++) {
    22	        var button = addToCartButtons[i]
    23	        button.addEventListener('click', addToCartClicked)
    24	    }
    25	
    26	    document.getElementsByClassName('btn-purchase')[0].addEventListener('click', purchaseClicked)
    27	}
    28	
    29	function purchaseClicked() {
    30	    alert('Thank you for your purchase')
    31	    var cartItems = document.getElementsByClassName('cart-items')[0]
    32	    while (cartItems.hasChildNodes()) {
    33	        cartItems.removeChild(cartItems.firstChild)
    34	    }
    35	    updateCartTotal()
    36	}
    37	
    38	function removeCartItem(event) {
    39	    var buttonClicked = event.target
    40	    buttonClicked.parentElement.parentElement.remove()
    41	    updateCartTotal()
    42	}
    43	
    44	function quantityChanged(event) {
    45	    var input = event.target
    46	    if (isNaN(input.value) || input.value <= 0) {
    47	        input.value = 1
    48	    }
    49	    updateCartTotal()
    50	}
    51	
    52	function addToCartClicked(event) {
    53	    var button = event.target
    54	    var shopItem = button.parentElement.parentElement
    55	    var title = shopItem.getElementsByClassName('shop-item-title')[0].innerText
    56	    var price = shopItem.getElementsByClassName('shop-item-price')[0].innerText
    57	    var imageSrc = shopItem.getElementsByClassName('shop-item-image')[0].src
    58	    addItemToCart(title, price, imageSrc)
    59	    updateCartTotal()
    60	}
    61	
    62	function addItemToCart(title, price, imageSrc) {
    63	    var cartRow = document.createElement('div')
    64	    cartRow.classList.add('cart-row')
    65	    var cartItems = document.getElementsByClassName('cart-items')[0]
    66	    var cartItemNames = cartItems.getElementsByClassName('cart-item-title')
    67	    for (var i = 0; i < cartItemNames.length; i++) {
    68	        if (cartItemNames[i].innerText == title) {
    69	            alert('This item is already added to the cart')
    70	            return
    71	        }
    72	    }
    73	    var cartRowContents = `
    74	        <div class="cart-item cart-column">
    75	            <img class="cart-item-image" src="${imageSrc}" width="100" height="100">
    76	            <span class="cart-item-title">${title}</span>
    77	        </div>
    78	        <span class="cart-price cart-column">${price}</span>
    79	        <div class="cart-quantity cart-column">
    80	            <input class="cart-quantity-input" type="number" value="1">
    81	            <button class="btn btn-danger" type="button">REMOVE</button>
    82	        </div>`
    83	    cartRow.innerHTML = cartRowContents
    84	    cartItems.append(cartRow)
    85	    cartRow.getElementsByClassName('btn-danger')[0].addEventListener('click', removeCartItem)
    86	    cartRow.getElementsByClassName('cart-quantity-input')[0].addEventListener('change', quantityChanged)
    87	}
    88	
    89	function updateCartTotal() {
    90	    var cartItemContainer = document.getElementsByClassName('cart-items')[0]
    91	    var cartRows = cartItemContainer.getElementsByClassName('cart-row')
    92	    var total = 0
    93	    for (var i = 0; i < cartRows.length; i++) {
    94	        var cartRow = cartRows[i]
    95	        var priceElement = cartRow.getElementsByClassName('cart-price')[0]
    96	        var quantityElement = cartRow.getElementsByClassName('cart-quantity-input')[0]
    97	        var price = parseFloat(priceElement.innerText.replace('$', ''))
    98	        var quantity = quantityElement.value
    99	        total = total + (price * quantity)
   100	    }
   101	    total = Math.round(total * 100) / 100
   102	    document.getElementsByClassName('cart-total-price')[0].innerText = '$' + total
   103	}

     1	@import url("https://fonts.googleapis.com/css?family=Open+Sans|Oswald:200");
     2	.parallax {
     3	  position: relative;
     4	  width: 100vw;
     5	  height: 50vw;
     6	  transform-origin: bottom center;
     7	  will-change: transform;
     8	  transform: translate3d(0, calc(var(--pct) * 35%), 0) scale3d(calc(var(--pct)/2 + 1), calc(var(--pct)/2 + 1), 1);
     9	}
    10	.parallax div {
    11	  position: absolute;
    12	  top: 0;
    13	  left: 0;
    14	  width: 100%;
    15	  height: 100%;
    16	  will-change: transform;
    17	  transform-origin: bottom center;
    18	  transform: translate3d(0, 0, 0);
    19	}
    20	.parallax div.p1 {
    21	  transform: translate3d(0, calc(var(--pct) * -20.0%), 0);
    22	}
    23	.parallax div.p2 {
    24	  transform: translate3d(0, calc(var(--pct) * -17.5%), 0);
    25	}
    26	.parallax div.p3 {
    27	  transform: translate3d(0, calc(var(--pct) * -15.0%), 0);

FILE: mob/utils/headerScrollContext.js
============================================================

     1	import { createContext, useContext } from "react";
     2	
     3	const HeaderScrollContext = createContext(null);
     4	
     5	export function HeaderScrollProvider({ children, scrollY }) {
     6	  return (
     7	    <HeaderScrollContext.Provider value={scrollY}>
     8	      {children}
     9	    </HeaderScrollContext.Provider>
    10	  );
    11	}
    12	
    13	export function useHeaderScrollY() {
    14	  return useContext(HeaderScrollContext);
    15	}

============================================================
FILE: mob/utils/openPaymentLink.js
============================================================

     1	import { Alert, Linking, Platform } from "react-native";
     2	
     3	export async function openPaymentLink(url) {
     4	  if (!url) {
     5	    if (Platform.OS === "web") {
     6	      window.alert("No payment link has been added for this product yet.");
     7	      return;
     8	    }
     9	
    10	    Alert.alert("Payment link missing", "No payment link has been added yet.");
    11	    return;
    12	  }
    13	
    14	  try {
    15	    const canOpen = await Linking.canOpenURL(url);
    16	
    17	    if (!canOpen) {
    18	      throw new Error("Cannot open URL");
    19	    }
    20	
    21	    await Linking.openURL(url);
    22	  } catch (error) {
    23	    if (Platform.OS === "web") {
    24	      window.alert("Unable to open this payment link.");
    25	      return;
    26	    }
    27	
    28	    Alert.alert("Unable to open link", "Please try again later.");
    29	  }
    30	}

FILE: other/script.js
============================================================

     1	"use strict";
     2	//  passive events 
     3	let passiveSupported = false;
     4	try {
     5	    window.addEventListener("test", null, Object.defineProperty({}, "passive", { get: function () { passiveSupported = true; } }));
     6	}
     7	catch (err) { }
     8	//  scroll listener
     9	addEventListener('scroll', e => {
    10	    const height = document.querySelector('.parallax').getBoundingClientRect().height;
    11	    const percentage = Math.min(Math.max(pageYOffset / height, 0), 1);
    12	    document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--pct', percentage);
    13	}, passiveSupported ? { passive: true } : false);
    14	//  link to open without iframe 
    15	const inIframe = () => { try {
    16	    return window.self !== window.top;
    17	}
    18	catch (e) {
    19	    return true;
    20	} };
    21	if (inIframe())
    22	    document.querySelector('.no-iframe').style.display = 'block';
    23	
    24	
    25	
    26	
    27	// contact form
    28	
    29	const form = document.getElementById("form");
    30	const result = document.getElementById("result");
    31	
    32	form.addEventListener("submit", function (e) {
    33	  const formData = new FormData(form);
    34	  e.preventDefault();
    35	  var object = {};
    36	  formData.forEach((value, key) => {
    37	    object[key] = value;
    38	  });
    39	  var json = JSON.stringify(object);
    40	  result.innerHTML = "Please wait...";
    41	
    42	  fetch("https://api.web3forms.com/submit", {
    43	    method: "POST",
    44	    headers: {
    45	      "Content-Type": "application/json",
    46	      Accept: "application/json"
    47	    },
    48	    body: json
    49	  })
    50	    .then(async (response) => {
    51	      let json = await response.json();
    52	      if (response.status == 200) {
    53	        result.innerHTML = json.message;
    54	        result.classList.remove("text-gray-500");
    55	        result.classList.add("text-green-500");
    56	      } else {
    57	        console.log(response);
    58	        result.innerHTML = json.message;
    59	        result.classList.remove("text-gray-500");
    60	        result.classList.add("text-red-500");
    61	      }
    62	    })
    63	    .catch((error) => {
    64	      console.log(error);
    65	      result.innerHTML = "Something went wrong!";
    66	    })
    67	    .then(function () {
    68	      form.reset();
    69	      setTimeout(() => {
    70	        result.style.display = "none";
    71	      }, 5000);
    72	    });
    73	});
    74	
    75	// end of contact form
    76

============================================================
FILE: other/shop.js
============================================================

     1	if (document.readyState == 'loading') {
     2	    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', ready)
     3	} else {
     4	    ready()
     5	}
     6	
     7	function ready() {
     8	    var removeCartItemButtons = document.getElementsByClassName('btn-danger')
     9	    for (var i = 0; i < removeCartItemButtons.length; i++) {
    10	        var button = removeCartItemButtons[i]
    11	        button.addEventListener('click', removeCartItem)
    12	    }
    13	
    14	    var quantityInputs = document.getElementsByClassName('cart-quantity-input')
    15	    for (var i = 0; i < quantityInputs.length; i++) {
    16	        var input = quantityInputs[i]
    17	        input.addEventListener('change', quantityChanged)
    18	    }
    19	
    20	    var addToCartButtons = document.getElementsByClassName('shop-item-button')
    21	    for (var i = 0; i < addToCartButtons.length; i++) {
    22	        var button = addToCartButtons[i]
    23	        button.addEventListener('click', addToCartClicked)
    24	    }
    25	
    26	    document.getElementsByClassName('btn-purchase')[0].addEventListener('click', purchaseClicked)
    27	}
    28	
    29	function purchaseClicked() {
    30	    alert('Thank you for your purchase')
    31	    var cartItems = document.getElementsByClassName('cart-items')[0]
    32	    while (cartItems.hasChildNodes()) {
    33	        cartItems.removeChild(cartItems.firstChild)
    34	    }
    35	    updateCartTotal()
    36	}
    37	
    38	function removeCartItem(event) {
    39	    var buttonClicked = event.target
    40	    buttonClicked.parentElement.parentElement.remove()
    41	    updateCartTotal()
    42	}
    43	
    44	function quantityChanged(event) {
    45	    var input = event.target
    46	    if (isNaN(input.value) || input.value <= 0) {
    47	        input.value = 1
    48	    }
    49	    updateCartTotal()
    50	}
    51	
    52	function addToCartClicked(event) {
    53	    var button = event.target
    54	    var shopItem = button.parentElement.parentElement
    55	    var title = shopItem.getElementsByClassName('shop-item-title')[0].innerText
    56	    var price = shopItem.getElementsByClassName('shop-item-price')[0].innerText
    57	    var imageSrc = shopItem.getElementsByClassName('shop-item-image')[0].src
    58	    addItemToCart(title, price, imageSrc)
    59	    updateCartTotal()
    60	}
    61	
    62	function addItemToCart(title, price, imageSrc) {
    63	    var cartRow = document.createElement('div')
    64	    cartRow.classList.add('cart-row')
    65	    var cartItems = document.getElementsByClassName('cart-items')[0]
    66	    var cartItemNames = cartItems.getElementsByClassName('cart-item-title')
    67	    for (var i = 0; i < cartItemNames.length; i++) {
    68	        if (cartItemNames[i].innerText == title) {
    69	            alert('This item is already added to the cart')
    70	            return
    71	        }
    72	    }
    73	    var cartRowContents = `
    74	        <div class="cart-item cart-column">
    75	            <img class="cart-item-image" src="${imageSrc}" width="100" height="100">
    76	            <span class="cart-item-title">${title}</span>
    77	        </div>
    78	        <span class="cart-price cart-column">${price}</span>
    79	        <div class="cart-quantity cart-column">
    80	            <input class="cart-quantity-input" type="number" value="1">
    81	            <button class="btn btn-danger" type="button">REMOVE</button>
    82	        </div>`
    83	    cartRow.innerHTML = cartRowContents
    84	    cartItems.append(cartRow)
    85	    cartRow.getElementsByClassName('btn-danger')[0].addEventListener('click', removeCartItem)
    86	    cartRow.getElementsByClassName('cart-quantity-input')[0].addEventListener('change', quantityChanged)
    87	}
    88	
    89	function updateCartTotal() {
    90	    var cartItemContainer = document.getElementsByClassName('cart-items')[0]
    91	    var cartRows = cartItemContainer.getElementsByClassName('cart-row')
    92	    var total = 0
    93	    for (var i = 0; i < cartRows.length; i++) {
    94	        var cartRow = cartRows[i]
    95	        var priceElement = cartRow.getElementsByClassName('cart-price')[0]
    96	        var quantityElement = cartRow.getElementsByClassName('cart-quantity-input')[0]
    97	        var price = parseFloat(priceElement.innerText.replace('$', ''))
    98	        var quantity = quantityElement.value
    99	        total = total + (price * quantity)
   100	    }
   101	    total = Math.round(total * 100) / 100
   102	    document.getElementsByClassName('cart-total-price')[0].innerText = '$' + total
   103	}
